\displaystyle\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\widetilde{x}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{l}}\in(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l-1}})}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l}})};\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{l+1}}\in(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l}})}x({x}_{i_{l+1}});\cdots

\displaystyle\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{k}];\big({y_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};y_{k}();\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}})};\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}})}x({x}_{i_{k}});

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}{{\displaystyle\llbracket}M[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{l}/{x}_{i_{l}}\}\big)\cdots\big))\cdots)\big)\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{1}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{l})({z_{l}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{l})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{l}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{l}}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\widetilde{x}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{l}}\in(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l-1}})}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus\widetilde{x})}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(z_{1},\cdots,z_{l})}.\mathtt{none})\cdots)\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{l}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})(\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\widetilde{x}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{l}}\in(\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l-1}})}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus\widetilde{x})}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\equiv\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus\widetilde{x})}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{7l+10}

The rest of the proof is by induction on the number of reductions
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{j}Q
.

i.

When
j=0
, the result follows trivially. Just take
{N}={N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q=Q^{\prime}
.

ii.

When
1\leq j\leq 7l+10
.

Let
Q_{j}
be such that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{j}Q_{j}
.
By the steps above one has

\begin{aligned} &Q_{j}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{7l+10-j}Q_{7l+6}=Q^{\prime},\\
&{N}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{1}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}={N}^{\prime};\text{ and}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{7l+10}.\end{aligned}

iii.

When
j>7l+10
.

In this case, we have

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{7l+10}Q_{7l+10}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{n}Q,
for
n\geq 1
.
We also know that

{N}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{1}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}
. However no further reductions can be performed.

(c)

When
\mathsf{size}(\widetilde{x})<\mathsf{size}(C)
, the proof proceeds similarly to the previous case.

5.

{N}=M{\langle\!|}C/\widetilde{x}{|\!\rangle}
.

In this case let us consider
C=\lbag M_{1}\rbag\cdot\cdots\cdot\lbag M_{k}\rbag
,

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\langle\!|}C/\widetilde{x}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

Therefore,

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}R\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{n}Q,

for some process
R
. Where
\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{n}
is a reduction that initially synchronizes with
{z_{i}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{i})}
for some
i\leq k
when
n\geq 1
,
n+m=k\geq 1
. Type preservation in
{{\mathsf{s}\pi\mkern-2.0mu}^{+}\mkern-1.0mu}
ensures reducing
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}
does not consume possible synchronizations with
\overline{z_{i}}.\mathtt{some}
, if they occur. Let us consider the the possible sizes of both
m
and
n
.

(a)

For
m=0
and
n\geq 1
.

We have that
R={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
as
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{0}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

Notice that there are two possibilities of having an unguarded
\overline{z_{i}}.\mathtt{some}
or
\overline{z_{i}}.\mathtt{none}
without internal reductions:

i.

M=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x},\widetilde{y}}
.

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x},\widetilde{y}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}\overline{\widetilde{x}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{y}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\}

\displaystyle)\cdots)

\displaystyle\equiv(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\mathbin{|}\overline{z_{1}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\cdots\mathbin{|}\overline{z_{k}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{y}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none})\cdots)

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{k}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{y}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}=Q^{\prime},

and no further reductions can be performed.
We also have that
{N}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{y}\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)}={N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{y}\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q^{\prime}
.

ii.

\mathsf{head}(M)={x}_{i}
.

Then we have the following

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{x}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{j}\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}

\displaystyle(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{i}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{i}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

Notice that multiple reductions can take place along any of the channels
z_{l}
. Let us consider for simplicity that
i=l=k
.

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

We show the full process of the reduction in this case for correctness. Applying multiple reductions of the form
[{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\rightsquigarrow}_{\nu}]
we obtain:

\begin{aligned} &(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\end{aligned}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}R_{k}

[{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\rightsquigarrow}_{\nu}]

\vdots

\begin{aligned} &(\bm{\nu}z_{2})({z_{2}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{2})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{2}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{2}}}\mathbin{|}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}\\
&\hskip 18.49988pt(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)\\
\end{aligned}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}R_{1}

[{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\rightsquigarrow}_{\nu}]

\begin{aligned} &(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)\\
\end{aligned}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}R_{1})

where
R_{i}=(\bm{\nu}z_{i})({z_{i}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{i})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{i}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{i}}}\mathbin{|}R_{i+1})
for

Hence we wish to show the following reduction:

\begin{aligned} &(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\\
&\qquad\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}\\
&\qquad(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\end{aligned}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}R_{k}

We apply the reduction rule

P\mathrel{\succeq_{z_{k}}}P^{\prime}

Q\mathrel{\succeq_{z_{k}}}Q^{\prime}

(\bm{\nu}z_{k})(P^{\prime}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime})\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}R_{k}

(\bm{\nu}z_{k})(P\mathbin{|}Q)\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}_{z_{k}}R_{k}

Where we take
P={z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}\mathrel{\succeq_{z_{k}}}{z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}=P^{\prime}

and
Notice that when

we have that the substitution
\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\}
takes place

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle=\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\}

\displaystyle=\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k-1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-2}}\}}(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{z_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[z_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k-1}/x_{i_{k-1}}\}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\}}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{k}}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k-1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-2}},x_{i_{k}}\}}

\displaystyle(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{x_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[x_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\succeq_{z_{k}}}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k-1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-2}}\}}(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})(\overline{z_{k}}.\mathtt{some};[z_{k}\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}j]\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k-1}/x_{i_{k-1}}\}

Hence we wish to show the following reduction

Hence we continue with the following reductions:

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k-1})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k-1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k-1}}}

In addition,

{N}=M{\langle\!|}C/\widetilde{x}{|\!\rangle}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M\{M_{k}/x_{k}\}{\langle\!|}C\setminus M_{k}/\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{k}{|\!\rangle}={M}^{\prime}
.

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M\{M_{k}/x_{k}\}{\langle\!|}C\setminus M_{k}/\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{k}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k-1})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k-1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k-1}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k-1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k-1}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-2}}\}}

\displaystyle(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{j}}\mathbin{|}P)\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k-1}/x_{i_{k-1}}\})\cdots)=Q_{2}

A.

When
n=1
:

Then,
Q=Q_{1}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{1}Q_{1}
. Also,

Q_{1}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{1}Q_{2}=Q^{\prime}
,
{N}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{1}M\{M_{k}/x_{k}\}{\langle\!|}C\setminus M_{k}/\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{k}{|\!\rangle}={N}^{\prime}
and

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M\{M_{k}/x_{k}\}{\langle\!|}C\setminus M_{k}/\widetilde{x}\setminus x_{k}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{2}
.

B.

When
n\geq 2
:

Then
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{2}Q_{2}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{l}Q
, for
l\geq 0
. Also,

{N}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}{M}^{\prime}
,
Q_{2}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
. By the induction hypothesis, there exist
Q^{\prime}
and
{N}^{\prime}
such that
Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{i}Q^{\prime}
,
{M}^{\prime}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{j}{N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q^{\prime}
. Finally,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{2}Q_{2}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{l}Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{i}Q^{\prime}
and
{N}\rightarrow{M}^{\prime}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{j}{N}^{\prime}
.

(b)

For
m\geq 1
and
n\geq 0
.

i.

When
n=0
.

Then

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}R\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}R
where

. By the IH there exist

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}R\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle=Q

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{i}(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}R^{\prime}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

\displaystyle=Q^{\prime}

{N}=M{\langle\!|}C/\widetilde{x}{|\!\rangle}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{j}M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/\widetilde{x}{|\!\rangle}={N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q^{\prime}

ii.

When
n\geq 1
.
Then
R
has an occurrence of an unguarded
\overline{x_{i}}.\mathtt{some}
or
\overline{x_{i}}.\mathtt{none}
, this case follows by IH.

6.

{N}=M\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil
.

In this case,

\begin{aligned} {{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}&=(\bm{\nu}x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}~{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}}).\end{aligned}

Then,

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{N}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}~{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})(R\mathbin{|}~{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{n}Q.

for some process
R
. Where
\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{n}
is a reduction initially synchronises with
{!}x^{!}(x_{i})
when
n\geq 1
,
n+m=k\geq 1
. Type preservation in
{{\mathsf{s}\pi\mkern-2.0mu}^{+}\mkern-1.0mu}
ensures reducing
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{m}
doesn’t consume possible synchronisations with
{!}x^{!}(x_{i})
if they occur. Let us consider the the possible sizes of both
m
and
n
.

(a)

For
m=0
and
n\geq 1
.

In this case,
R={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
as
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{0}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

Notice that the only possibility of having an unguarded
{?}\overline{x^{!}}[x_{i}]
without internal reductions is when
\mathsf{head}(M)={x}[j]
for some index

0.G.4
Success Sensitivity

Proposition 4

(Preservation of Success)

For all closed
M\in\lambda_{\mathtt{C}}
, the following hold:

1.

\mathsf{head}(M)=\checkmark\implies{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{x})(P\mathbin{|}\checkmark)

2.

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=P\mathbin{|}\checkmark\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}Q\implies\mathsf{head}(M)=\checkmark

Proof.

Proof of both cases by induction on the structure of
M
.

1.

We only need to consider terms of the following form:

(a)

M=\checkmark
:

This case is immediate.

(b)

M=N\ (C\star U)
:

Then,
\mathsf{head}(N\ (C\star U))=\mathsf{head}(N)
. If
\mathsf{head}(N)=\checkmark
, then

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M(C\star U){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})).

By the IH,
\checkmark
is unguarded in
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

(c)

M=M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}{|\!\rangle}

Then we have that
\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}{|\!\rangle})=\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime})=\checkmark
. Then

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{1}}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{{z_{k}}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\}}\cdots\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{k}}\in\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\setminus x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{k-1}}\}}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{k}/x_{i_{k}}\})\cdots)

and by the IH
\checkmark
is unguarded in
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
hence ungaurded in every summand in
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

(d)

M=M^{\prime}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil

Then we have that
\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil)=\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime})=\checkmark
. Then
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})
and by the IH
\checkmark
is unguarded in
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

2.

We only need to consider terms of the following form:

(a)

Case
M=\checkmark
:

Then,

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\checkmark{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=\checkmark

which is an unguarded occurrence of

and that

(b)

Theorem 0.G.4

(Success Sensitivity (Under
\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}
))

{{M}}\Downarrow{\checkmark}
if and only if
{{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\Downarrow}}_{\checkmark}
for well-formed closed terms
M
.

Proof.

We proceed with the proof in two parts.

1.

Suppose that
{M}\Downarrow_{\checkmark}
. We will prove that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\Downarrow}}_{\checkmark}
.

By Def.
17
, there exists
{M}^{\prime}
such that
M\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}{M}^{\prime}
and

\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime})=\checkmark
.
By completeness, if
M\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M^{\prime}
then there exist
Q,Q^{\prime}
such that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\equiv Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\equiv Q^{\prime}
.

We wish to show that there exists
Q^{\prime\prime}
such that
Q^{\prime}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q^{\prime\prime}
and
Q^{\prime\prime}
has an unguarded occurrence of
\checkmark
.

By Proposition
4
(1) we have that
\mathsf{head}(M^{\prime})=\checkmark\implies{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{x})(P\mathbin{|}\checkmark)
. Finally
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{x})(P\mathbin{|}\checkmark)\equiv Q^{\prime}
. Hence
Q
reduces to a process that has an unguarded occurrence of
\checkmark
.

2.

Suppose that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\Downarrow}}_{\checkmark}
. We will prove that
{M}\Downarrow_{\checkmark}
.

By operational soundness we have that if
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q
then there exist
{M}^{\prime}
and
Q^{\prime}
such that:
(i)
{M}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}{M}^{\prime}

and
(ii)
Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q^{\prime}
with

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\equiv Q^{\prime}
.

Since
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q
, and
Q=Q^{\prime\prime}\mathbin{|}\checkmark
and
Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q^{\prime}
we must have that
Q^{\prime\prime}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.38,0.61,0.27}\leadsto}}^{*}Q^{\prime\prime\prime}
with
Q^{\prime}=Q^{\prime\prime\prime}\mathbin{|}\checkmark
. As
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\equiv Q^{\prime}
we have that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=
. Finally applying Proposition
4
(2) we have that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}{M}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q^{\prime\prime\prime}\mathbin{|}\checkmark
is itself a term with unguarded
\checkmark
, then
{M}
is itself headed with
\checkmark
.
∎

Appendix 0.H
Proof of Loose Correctness of the Translation under the Eager Semantics

0.H.1
Completeness

See
0.B.3

Proof.

By induction on the reduction rule applied to infer
N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M
.
We have five cases.

1.

Case
\mathtt{[RS:Beta]}
:
Then
N=(\lambda x.(M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]))B\mathbin{\longrightarrow}(M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$B$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle=M
, where
B=C\star U
. The result folows easily, since

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\lambda x.(M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

0.H.2
Soundness

We define
P\mathbin{\longrightarrow}\{P_{i}\}_{i\in I}
, for a fixed finite set
I
where
I=\{i\ s.t.\ P\mathbin{\longrightarrow}P_{i}\}
. Similarly we define
P\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}\{P_{i}\}_{i\in I}
to be defined inductively by
P\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}\{P_{i}\}_{i\in I}
and
P_{i}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}\{P_{j}\}_{j\in J_{i}}
for each
i\in I
then
P\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}\{P_{j}\}_{j\in J}
with
J=\cup_{i\in I}J_{i}

See
2

Proof.

Proof by induction on the precongruence rules.

•

When
P=P\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}P=Q
then all reductions in
P
are matched in
Q
.

•

When
P=P_{1}\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}P_{2}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q

. Let us take

Then by the rule:

and
P_{1}\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}P_{2}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}P_{1}\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\{P^{\prime}_{2_{i}}\}_{i\in I_{2}}

for some
I_{1},I_{2}
Hence we have that
P_{1}\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}P_{2}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}\{P^{\prime}_{i}\}_{i\in I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
where

if

. By the induction hypothesis
P_{1}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q

,

and

. We take
J=J^{\prime}
and hence we can deduce
P\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}\{P^{\prime}_{i}\}_{i\in I_{1}\cup I_{2}}
,
Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{Q_{j}\}_{j\in J}
. Finally we have that
\forall j\in J
.

P_{1_{j}}^{\prime}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{j}

P_{1_{j}}^{\prime}\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}P_{2}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q

When
P=P_{1}\mathbin{|}P_{2}
with

. Then by the rule:

[{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\shortrightarrow}_{\mathbin{|}}]

When

with

See
1

Proof.

By induction on the structure of
N
and then induction on the number of reductions of
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}Q
.

1.

Base case:

N={x}
,
N={x}[j]
,
N=\mathtt{fail}^{\emptyset}
and
N=\lambda x.(M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])
.
.

No reductions can take place, and the result follows trivially. Take
I=\{a\}
,

Q={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{0}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{a}
and
{x}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{0}{x}=N^{\prime}
.

2.

N=M^{\prime}(C\star U)
.

Then,

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}(C\star U){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))
, and we are able to perform the reductions from
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}(C\star U){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
.

We now proceed by induction on
k
, with
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{k}Q
. There are two main cases:

(a)

When
k=0
the thesis follows easily:

We have
i=\{a\}
,

Q={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}(C\star U){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{0}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}(C\star U){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{a}
and
M^{\prime}(C\star U)\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{0}M^{\prime}(C\star U)=N^{\prime}
.

(b)

The interesting case is when
k\geq 1
.

Then, for some process
R
and
n,m
such that
k=n+m
, we have the following:

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{m}(\bm{\nu}v)(R\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{n}Q

Thus, the first
m\geq 0
reduction steps are internal to
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}
; type preservation in
{{\mathsf{s}\pi\mkern-2.0mu}^{+}\mkern-1.0mu}
ensures that, if they occur, these reductions do not discard the possibility of synchronizing with
\overline{v}.\mathtt{some}
. Then, the first of the
n\geq 0
reduction steps towards
Q
is a synchronization between
R
and
{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)}
.

We consider two sub-cases, depending on the values of
m
and
n
:

(b.1) Case
m=0
and
n\geq 1
:

Then
R={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}
as
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{0}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}
.
Notice that there are two possibilities of having an unguarded:

i.

M^{\prime}=(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])){\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil\quad(p,q\geq 0)

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])){\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}

For simplicity we shall denote

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])){\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})

where
\widetilde{y}=\widetilde{y_{1}},\cdots,\widetilde{y_{p}}
.
\widetilde{z}=z^{!}_{1},\cdots,z^{!}_{q}
and we continue the evaluation as:

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})(\overline{v}.\mathtt{some};v(x);\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})

With this shape for
M
, we then have the following:

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}(M^{\prime}\ B){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}v)((\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})(v(x);\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})\mathbin{|}\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle=Q_{1}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x)((\bm{\nu}v)((\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})\mathbin{|}[v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u])\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

\displaystyle=Q_{2}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x)((\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

\displaystyle=Q_{3}

We also have that

\displaystyle N

\displaystyle=(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])){\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil(C\star U)

\displaystyle\equiv_{\lambda}(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])(C\star U)){\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil

\displaystyle\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$(C\star U)$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil=M

Furthermore, we have:

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}{{\displaystyle\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$(C\star U)$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x)((\bm{\nu}\widetilde{y},\widetilde{z})(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}Q^{\prime\prime})\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

We consider different possibilities for
n\geq 1
; in all the cases, the result follows.

When
n=1
:

We have
I=\{a\}
,
Q=Q_{1}
,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{1}Q_{1}
.
We also have that

•

Q_{1}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{2}Q_{3}=Q_{a}
,

•

N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{1}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$(C\star U)$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil=N^{\prime}

•

and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$(C\star U)$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\langle\!|}C_{1}/\widetilde{y_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{p}/\widetilde{y_{p}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{3}
.

When
n=2,3
:
the analysis is similar.

When
n\geq 4
:

We have
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{3}Q_{3}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}Q
. We also know that
N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M
,
Q_{3}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
. By the IH, there exist
\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I}
,
N^{\prime}
such that
Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I}
,
M\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}N^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{i}\quad\forall i\in I
. Finally,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{3}Q_{3}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{l}Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}_{I}Q_{i}
and
N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}N^{\prime}
.

ii.

M^{\prime}=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}
.

Then,
\begin{aligned} {{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}&={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}=\overline{v}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{z}}.\mathtt{none}.\end{aligned}

With this shape for
M
, we have:

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}(M^{\prime}\ (C\star U)){\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}v)(\overline{v}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{z}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{z}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}

We also have that

N=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}}\ C\star U\mathbin{\longrightarrow}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)}=N^{\prime}.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\widetilde{z}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}.

(b.2) Case
m\geq 1
and
n\geq 0
:

We distinguish two cases:

i.

When
n=0
:

Then,
(\bm{\nu}v)(R\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))=Q
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{m}R
where
m\geq 1
. Then by the IH there exist
\{R_{i}^{\prime}\}_{i\in I}
and
M^{\prime\prime}
such that
R\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{R_{i}^{\prime}\}_{i\in I}
,
M^{\prime}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}M^{\prime\prime}
, and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}R_{i}\quad\forall i\in I
. Hence we have that

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}v)({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{m}(\bm{\nu}v)(R\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))=Q

We also know that

\displaystyle Q

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{(\bm{\nu}v)(R^{\prime}_{i}\mathbin{|}{v.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}[x].([v\mathbin{\leftrightarrow}u]\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x}))\}_{i\in I}=\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I}

and so the
\lambda_{\mathtt{C}}
term can reduce as follows:
N=(M^{\prime}\ (C\star U))\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}M^{\prime\prime}\ (C\star U)=N^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{i}\quad\forall i\in I
via the
\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}
rules.

ii.

When
n\geq 1
:

Then
R
has an occurrence of an unguarded
\overline{v}.\mathtt{some}
or
\overline{v}.\mathtt{none}
, hence it is of the form

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}(\lambda x.(M^{\prime\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])){\langle\!|}N_{1}/y_{1}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}N_{p}/y_{p}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U_{1}/z^{!}_{1}\rrceil\cdots\llfloor U_{q}/z^{!}_{q}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}
or
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{v}.

This concludes the analysis for the case
N=(M^{\prime}\,(C\star U))
.

3.

N=M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]
.
The sharing variable
{x}
is not free and the result follows by vacuity.

4.

N=M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle
. Then we have

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=(\bm{\nu}x)(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

Let us consider three cases.

(a)

When
\mathsf{size}({\widetilde{x}})=\mathsf{size}(C)
.
Then let us consider the shape of the bag
C
.

i.

When
C=\mathtt{1}
.

We have the following

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x)(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x)(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{x.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x}[x^{\ell}].

\displaystyle({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[x^{!}].({!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[])))

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x)(x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[x^{\ell}].({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[x^{!}].({!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[])))

\displaystyle=Q_{1}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x,x^{\ell})(x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[x^{!}].({!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[]))

\displaystyle=Q_{2}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x,x^{\ell},x^{!})(x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}}\mathbin{|}\overline{x}[])

\displaystyle=Q_{3}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=Q_{4}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!})(\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{i}].({y_{i}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})};y_{i}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{n});(\overline{y_{n}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{n}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!})(\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{i}].({y_{i}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})};y_{i}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle x^{\ell}(y_{n});(\overline{y_{n}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{n}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=Q_{5}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!},y_{i})({y_{i}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})};y_{i}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{y_{i}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{i}}[]

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=Q_{6}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!},y_{i})(y_{i}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{y_{i}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=Q_{7}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{\ell},x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=Q_{8}

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=Q_{9}

Notice how
Q_{8}
has a choice however the
x^{\ell}
name can be closed at any time so for simplicity we only perform communication across this name once all other names have completed their reductions.

Now we proceed by induction on the number of reductions
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{k}Q
.

A.

When
k=0
, the result follows trivially. Just take
I=\{a\}
,
\mathbb{N}=\mathbb{N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q=Q_{a}
.

B.

When
k=1
.

We have
Q=Q_{1}
,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{1}Q_{1}
. Let us take
I=a
, we also have that
Q_{1}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{8}Q_{9}=Q_{a}
,
N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}M^{\prime}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil=M
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{9}

C.

When
2\leq k\leq 8
.

Proceeds similarly to the previous case

D.

When
k\geq 9
.

We have
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{9}Q_{9}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{l}Q
, for
l\geq 1
. Since
Q_{9}={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}
we apply the induction hypothesis we have that there
\exists\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I}
,
N^{\prime}\ s.t.\ Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I},M^{\prime}\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}N^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{i}\quad\forall i\in I
. Then,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{9}Q_{9}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{l}Q\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{Q_{i}\}_{i\in I}
and by the contextual reduction rule it follows that
N=(M^{\prime}[\leftarrow x])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$1$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}N^{\prime}
and the case holds.

ii.

When
C=\lbag N_{1}\rbag\cdot\cdots\cdot\lbag N_{l}\rbag
, for
l\geq 1
.
Then,

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x)(\overline{x}.\mathtt{some};x(x^{\ell});x(x^{!});x();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{4}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}x^{\ell})({{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x^{\ell}})\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}x^{\ell})(\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{1}];\big({y_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};y_{1}();\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})\setminus\widetilde{x}};\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{1}}\in\widetilde{x}}x({x}_{i_{1}});\cdots

\displaystyle\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{l}];\big({y_{l}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};y_{l}();\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})\setminus(\widetilde{x}\setminus(x_{i_{1}},\cdots,x_{i_{l-1}}))};\scalebox{1.5}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}\mkern-3.0mu$}_{x_{i_{l}}\in\widetilde{x}}x({x}_{i_{l}});

\displaystyle\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{l+1}];({y_{l+1}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})};y_{l+1}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})\big)\cdots\big)

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};x(y_{1});{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{y_{1},\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[z_{1}];

\displaystyle({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{1}}\mathbin{|}\overline{y_{1}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle\cdots{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{l})};x(y_{l});{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{y_{l},\mathsf{lfv}(M_{l})};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[z_{l}];

\displaystyle({z_{l}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{l})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{l}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{l}}\mathbin{|}\overline{y_{l}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};x(y_{l+1});(\overline{y_{l+1}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{l+1}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}))\cdots))\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{6l}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}x^{\ell})((\bm{\nu}z_{1})(\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{l})(\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{x^{\ell}}[y_{l+1}].({y_{l+1}.\mathtt{some}}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})};

\displaystyle y_{l+1}();{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{l}/x_{i_{l}}\}\mathbin{|}\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none})

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}{z_{l}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{j_{l}})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{j_{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{l}})\cdots\mathbin{|}{z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{j_{1}})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{j_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{1}})\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{l+1}).(\overline{y_{l+1}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{l+1}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}))\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{5}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}z_{1})({x_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{j_{1}})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{j_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{1}}\mathbin{|}\cdots

\displaystyle(\bm{\nu}z_{l})({x_{l}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{j_{l}})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{j_{l}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{l}}\mathbin{|}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\{z_{1}/x_{i_{1}}\}\cdots\{z_{l}/x_{i_{l}}\})\cdots)\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

The proof follows by induction on the number of reductions
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{k}Q
.

A.

When
k=0
, the result follows trivially. Just take
I=\{a\}
,
\mathbb{N}=\mathbb{N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q=Q_{a}
.

B.

When
1\leq k\leq 6l+9
.

I=\{a\}
,
Q_{k}
be such that

.
We also have that
Q_{k}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{6l+9-k}Q_{6l+9}=Q_{a}
,

C.

When

Then,
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{6l+9}Q_{6l+9}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{n}Q

\exists\{P_{j}\}_{j\in J},N^{\prime},\ s.t.\ M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{l}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{*}N^{\prime},

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{l}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}P_{j}\quad\forall j\in J
. We also have by Prop.
2
that as
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{l}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{6l+9}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{l}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U/x^{!}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{*}\{Q_{j}\}_{j\in J}

,

and
P_{i}\succeq_{\mkern-2.0mu\scalebox{.8}{$\mathbin{\hbox{\hbox to0.0pt{$\mkern-0.4mu{}\mathbin{|\mkern-3.0mu|}{}$\hss}$-$}}$}}Q_{i}\ ,\ \forall i\in I

(b)

When
\mathsf{size}(\widetilde{x})>\mathsf{size}(C)
.

Then we have

and

for some

\displaystyle{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};x(y_{l+1});(\overline{y_{l+1}}.\mathtt{some};\overline{y_{l+1}}[]\mathbin{|}{x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}}_{\emptyset};\overline{x^{\ell}}.\mathtt{none}))\cdots))\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{6}(\bm{\nu}x^{!})((\bm{\nu}z_{1})({z_{1}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{1})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{1}}\mathbin{|}\cdots(\bm{\nu}z_{k})({z_{k}.\mathtt{some}}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M_{k})};{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}M_{k}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{z_{k}}\mathbin{|}

\displaystyle\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})\setminus\widetilde{x})}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{z_{1}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\cdots\mathbin{|}\overline{z_{k}}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(C)\setminus(M_{1},\cdots,M_{k}))}.\mathtt{none})\cdots)

\displaystyle\mathbin{|}{!}x^{!}(x_{i});{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{k}\overline{u}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{(\mathsf{lfv}(M^{\prime})\setminus\{x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}\})}.\mathtt{none}\mathbin{|}\overline{\mathsf{lfv}(C)}.\mathtt{none}

\displaystyle={{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{7l+10}

The rest of the proof is by induction on the number of reductions
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{j}Q
.

i.

When
j=0
, the result follows trivially. Just take
I=\{a\}

\mathbb{N}=\mathbb{N}^{\prime}
and
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q=Q_{a}
.

ii.

When
1\leq j\leq 7k+10
.

Let
I=\{a\}
and
Q_{j}
be such that
{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{j}Q_{j}
.
By the steps above one has

\begin{aligned} &Q_{j}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{7k+10-j}Q_{7k+10}=Q_{a},\\
&N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{1}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}=N^{\prime};\text{ and}{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}=Q_{7k+10}.\end{aligned}

iii.

When
j>7k+10
.

In this case, we have

{{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0.85,0,0.42}\rrbracket}}_{u}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{7k+10}Q_{7k+10}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.6,0,0.01}\longrightarrow}}^{n}Q,
for
n\geq 1
.
We also know that

N\mathbin{\longrightarrow}^{1}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{z}}
. However no further reductions can be performed.

(c)

When
\mathsf{size}(\widetilde{x})<\mathsf{size}(C)
, the proof proceeds similarly to the previous case.

5.

N=M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}C/x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}{|\!\rangle}

In this case we let
